#Total Number of Duplicate/Locked Missions: 40
ab_mop_misc_1 = {
	slot = 1
	generic = no
	ai = yes
	potential = {
		have_access_to_peasant_moldavia_missions = yes
	}
	mop_establish_league = {
		icon = mission_diplomatic_relation
		position = 1
		provinces_to_highlight = {
			is_capital = yes
			owner = {
				has_radicalized_peasantry_country = yes
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_debug_mode_enabled = yes
				}
				always = yes
			}
			else = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = {
						total_commune_development = 100
					}
					calc_true_if = {
						amount = 2
						custom_trigger_tooltip = {
							tooltip = commune_subject_amount_trigger_tt
							all_subject_country = {
								is_commune_subject = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			define_advisor = {
				type = statesman
				skill = 1
				cost_multiplier = 0.5
			}
			add_prestige_or_monarch_power = {
				amount = 10
			}
			add_faction_influence = {
				faction = leaguists_faction
				influence = 10
			}
		}
	}
	mop_strengthen_league_ties = {
		icon = personal_diplomacy
		position = 2
		required_missions = {
			mop_establish_league
		}
		provinces_to_highlight = {
			is_capital = yes
			owner = {
				is_subject_of_type_with_overlord = {
					who = root
					type = allied_commune
				}
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_debug_mode_enabled = yes
				}
				always = yes
			}
			else = {
				calc_true_if = {
					amount = 4
					custom_trigger_tooltip = {
						tooltip = mop_strengthen_league_ties_tt
						all_subject_country = {
							is_subject_of_type = integrated_commune
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			custom_tooltip = peasant_zeal_on_commune_integration_tt
			set_country_flag = peasant_zeal_on_commune_integration
			custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_modifier = annexers_legacy
				}
				custom_tooltip = LIT_INTEGRATE_YES
				hidden_effect = {
					remove_country_modifier = annexers_legacy
				}
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = LIT_INTEGRATE_NO
			}
			custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY
			add_republican_tradition = 5
			change_peasant_zeal = {
				value = 10
			}
			add_faction_influence = {
				faction = leaguists_faction
				influence = 15
			}
		}
	}
	mop_league_integration = {
		icon = GFX_mop_league_integration
		position = 3
		required_missions = {
			mop_strengthen_league_ties
		}
		provinces_to_highlight = {

			owner = {
				is_subject_of_type_with_overlord = {

		}

		}

				value = 20
			}

				faction = leaguists_faction
				influence = 20
			}
		}
	}
	mop_internationalism = {
		icon = mission_explore_the_world
		position = 4
		required_missions = {
			mop_league_integration
		}
		provinces_to_highlight = {

ab_mop_misc_2 = {
	slot = 2
	generic = no
	ai = yes
	potential = {
		have_access_to_peasant_moldavia_missions = yes
	}
	mop_seat_of_the_metropolitan = {
		icon = gfx_wal_seat_of_the_metropolitan
		position = 1
		provinces_to_highlight = {
			province_id = 1756
			OR = {
				NOT = {
					country_or_non_sovereign_subject_holds = ROOT
				}
				NOT = {
					num_of_times_improved = 3
				}
				NOT = {
					has_tax_building_trigger = yes
				}
				has_state_patriach = no
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_debug_mode_enabled = yes
				}
				always = yes
			}
			else = {
				owns_core_province = 1756
				1756 = {
					has_state_patriach = yes
					has_tax_building_trigger = yes
					num_of_times_improved = 3
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			1756 = {
				add_province_modifier = {
					name = wallachia_tirgoviste_metropolitan
					duration = 5475
				}
			}
			add_patriarch_authority = 0.05
			add_estate_loyalty = {
				estate = estate_church
				loyalty = 10
			}
			add_faction_influence = {
				faction = exemplarists_faction
				influence = 10
			}
		}
	}
	mop_romanian_churches = {
		icon = gfx_wal_romanian_churches
		position = 2
		required_missions = {
			mop_seat_of_the_metropolitan
		}
		provinces_to_highlight = {
			owned_by = ROOT
			culture_group = romanian_culture_group
			OR = {
				has_tax_building_trigger = no
				NOT = {
					num_of_times_improved = 6
				}
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_debug_mode_enabled = yes
				}
				always = yes
			}
			else = {
				1756 = {
					owned_by = ROOT
					has_tax_building_trigger = yes
					num_of_times_improved = 6
				}
				calc_true_if = {
					amount = 6
					all_owned_province = {
						culture_group = romanian_culture_group
						has_tax_building_trigger = yes
						num_of_times_improved = 6
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			1756 = {
				upgrade_tax_building = yes
				add_province_modifier = {
					name = wallachia_tirgoviste_metropolitan_cathedral
					duration = -1
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = mop_romanian_churches_province_reward_tooltip
			tooltip = {
				add_patriarch_authority = 0.01
				add_province_modifier = {
					name = wallachia_pillar_of_the_faith
					duration = -1
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				every_owned_province = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							province_id = 1756
						}
						culture_group = romanian_culture_group
						has_tax_building_trigger = yes
						num_of_times_improved = 6
					}
					add_province_modifier = {
						name = wallachia_pillar_of_the_faith
						duration = -1
					}
					owner = {
						add_patriarch_authority = 0.01
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = buffed_development_of_temples
			custom_tooltip = buffed_development_of_temples_tooltip
			hidden_effect = {
				every_owned_province = {
					limit = {
						has_province_modifier = estate_church_development_of_temples_mod
					}
					remove_province_modifier = estate_church_development_of_temples_mod
					add_province_modifier = {
						name = buffed_estate_church_development_of_temples_mod
						duration = -1
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY
			add_patriarch_authority = 0.05
			add_estate_loyalty = {
				estate = estate_church
				loyalty = 15
			}
			add_faction_influence = {
				faction = exemplarists_faction
				influence = 10
			}
		}
	}
}

						any_province = {
							culture = bulgarian
							region = balkan_region
							owner = {
								OR = {
									is_commune_subject = no

								}

									tag = ROOT

							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {

				custom_tooltip = eu_annexed_yes
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = eu_annexed_no
			}

				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = annexers_legacy
					}
					remove_country_modifier = annexers_legacy
				}
				else = {
					add_dip_power = 50
				}
			}
			add_dip_power = 100
			add_faction_influence = {
				faction = leaguists_faction
				influence = 10
			}
		}

				first_limit = "is_bogdan_razesul_ruling = yes"
				first_effect = "
					change_peasant_zeal = {
						value = 100
					}
					add_faction_influence = {
						faction = abolitionists_faction
						influence = 20
					}
					add_estate_loyalty = {
						estate = estate_razesi
						loyalty = 20
					}
					add_country_modifier = {
						name = mop_the_legend_of_bogdan_razesul
						duration = -1
					}"
				second_custom_tooltip = if_bogdan_razesul_isnt_ruling_tt
				second_limit = "is_bogdan_razesul_ruling = no"
				second_effect = "
					change_peasant_zeal = {
						value = 50
					}
					add_faction_influence = {
						faction = abolitionists_faction
						influence = 10
					}
					add_estate_loyalty = {
						estate = estate_razesi
						loyalty = 10
					}"
			}
			custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY
			add_country_modifier = {
				name = mop_balkan_liberator
				duration = 36500
			}
			add_prestige_or_monarch_power = {
				amount = 25
			}
		}
	}

	ai = yes

	}
	mop_north_italy_nw = {
		icon = mission_conquer_north_italy
		position = 25
		provinces_to_highlight = {
			OR = {
				area = po_valley_area
				area = lombardy_area
				area = piedmont_area
				area = liguria_area
			}
			NOT = {
				country_or_commune_subject_or_subject_of_commune_subject_holds = {
					who = root
				}
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_debug_mode_enabled = yes
				}
				always = yes
			}
			else = {
				po_valley_area = {
					type = all
					country_or_commune_subject_or_subject_of_commune_subject_holds = {
						who = root
					}
				}
				lombardy_area = {
					type = all
					country_or_commune_subject_or_subject_of_commune_subject_holds = {
						who = root
					}
				}
				piedmont_area = {
					type = all
					country_or_commune_subject_or_subject_of_commune_subject_holds = {
						who = root
					}
				}
				liguria_area = {
					type = all
					country_or_commune_subject_or_subject_of_commune_subject_holds = {
						who = root
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			custom_tooltip = integrated_commune_lombard_bonus_tt
			set_country_flag = integrated_commune_lombard_bonus_flag
			custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY
			custom_tooltip = integrated_commune_piedmontese_bonus_tt
			set_country_flag = integrated_commune_piedmontese_bonus_flag
			custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY
			custom_tooltip = integrated_commune_ligurian_bonus_tt
			set_country_flag = integrated_commune_ligurian_bonus_flag
			custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY

				province = 101

				culture = ligurian

				location = 101

				cost_multiplier = 0.25
			}
			custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY
			add_years_of_income = 1
			change_peasant_zeal = {
				value = 20
			}
			add_faction_influence = {
				faction = abolitionists_faction
				influence = 20
			}
			unlock_mop_france_south_effect = yes
			unlock_mop_swiss_effect = yes
		}
	}

mop_germany_gpw = {
	slot = 3
	generic = no
	potential = {
		have_access_to_peasant_moldavia_missions = yes
		OR = {
			has_country_flag = unlocked_mop_austria
			has_country_flag = unlocked_mop_swiss
			has_country_flag = unlocked_mop_bavaria
			has_country_flag = unlocked_mop_swabia_franconia
			has_country_flag = unlocked_mop_bohemia
			has_country_flag = unlocked_mop_rhine
			has_country_flag = unlocked_mop_saxons
			has_country_flag = unlocked_mop_low_countries
		}
	}
	mop_great_peasants_war = {
		icon = GFX_mop_great_peasants_war
		position = 29
		provinces_to_highlight = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						hre_size = 1
					}
				}
				always = no
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_great_peasants_war_ended = yes
					NOT = {
						has_global_flag = great_peasants_war_flag
					}
				}
				always = no
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = great_peasants_war_flag
				}
				is_capital = yes
				is_part_of_hre = yes
				owner = {
					is_peasant_republic = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				is_capital = yes
				is_part_of_hre = yes
				owner = {
					is_peasant_republic = no
				}
			}
		}
		trigger = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					mission_completed = mop_great_peasants_war
				}
				always = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					is_debug_mode_enabled = yes
				}
				always = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						hre_size = 1
					}
				}
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = hre_was_dismantled_trigger_tooltip
					always = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_great_peasants_war_ended = yes
					NOT = {
						has_global_flag = great_peasants_war_flag
					}
				}
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = great_peasants_war_late_tt
					always = yes
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = great_peasants_war_flag
				}
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = have_invited_3_hre_countries_to_league_tt
					check_variable = {
						hre_nations_diplo_annexed = 3
					}
				}
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = has_supported_2_mass_peasant_uprisings_tt
					check_variable = {
						number_of_mass_peasant_uprisings = 2
					}
				}
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = incited_peasants_in_hre_tt
					calc_true_if = {
						all_country = {
							OR = {
								has_country_modifier = incited_peasantry
								has_country_modifier = incited_peasantry_great_peasants_war
							}
							is_part_of_hre = yes
						}
						amount = 4
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				dip_power = 100
				mil_power = 100
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = has_scornfully_insulted_the_hremperor_tt
					emperor = {
						has_opinion_modifier = {
							who = ROOT
							modifier = scornfully_insulted
						}
					}
				}
				has_spy_network_in = {
					who = emperor
					value = 50
				}
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = liberated_peasants_in_hre_tt
					check_variable = {
						hre_nations_liberated = 2
					}
				}
				custom_trigger_tooltip = {
					tooltip = incited_peasants_in_hre_tt
					calc_true_if = {
						all_country = {
							OR = {
								has_country_modifier = incited_peasantry
								has_country_modifier = incited_peasantry_great_peasants_war
							}
							is_part_of_hre = yes
						}
						amount = 4
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					mission_completed = mop_great_peasants_war
					has_global_flag = great_peasants_war_flag
				}
				custom_tooltip = gpw_basics_tt
				custom_tooltip = gpw_new_icon_tt
				custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY
				custom_tooltip = incite_peasants_gpw_changes_tt
				custom_tooltip = incite_peasants_verbose_gpw_changes_tt
				tooltip = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							hre_size = 1
						}
						emperor = {
							add_country_modifier = {
								name = incited_peasantry_great_peasants_war
								duration = 3650
							}
						}
					}
					else = {
						add_country_modifier = {
							name = incited_peasantry_great_peasants_war
							duration = 3650
						}
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = gpw_unlock_stage_mass_uprising_tt
				custom_tooltip = gpw_unlock_stage_mass_uprising_verbose_tt
				custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY
				custom_tooltip = gpw_opportunity_tt
				custom_tooltip = gpw_opportunity_verbose_tt
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						hre_size = 1
					}
				}
				add_mil_power = 50
				add_dip_power = 50
				add_adm_power = 50
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_great_peasants_war_ended = yes
					NOT = {
						has_global_flag = great_peasants_war_flag
					}
				}
				add_mil_power = 25
				add_dip_power = 25
				add_adm_power = 25
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = great_peasants_war_flag
				}
				add_country_modifier = {
					name = mop_great_peasants_war_mod
					duration = 3650
				}
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = incited_peasantry
						is_part_of_hre = yes
					}
					add_spy_network_from = {
						who = ROOT
						value = 75
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY
				change_peasant_zeal = {
					value = 20
				}
				add_faction_influence = {
					faction = abolitionists_faction
					influence = 10
				}
				add_faction_influence = {
					faction = leaguists_faction
					influence = 10
				}
			}
			else = {
				custom_tooltip = start_gpw_early_tt
				custom_tooltip = AB_EMPTY
				add_country_modifier = {
					name = mop_great_peasants_war_mod
					duration = 3650
				}
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						has_country_modifier = incited_peasantry
						is_part_of_hre = yes
					}
					custom_tooltip = mop_great_peasants_war_each_incited_country_tt
					tooltip = {
						add_country_modifier = {
							name = incited_peasantry_great_peasants_war
							duration = 3650
						}
					}
					hidden_effect = {
						remove_country_modifier = incited_peasantry
						add_country_modifier = {
							name = incited_peasantry_great_peasants_war
							duration = 3650
						}
						add_spy_network_from = {
							who = ROOT
							value = 75
						}
					}
				}
				add_spy_network_in = {
					who = emperor
					value = -50
				}
				emperor = {
					add_country_modifier = {
						name = incited_peasantry
						duration = 3650
					}
					capital_scope = {
						add_province_modifier = {
							name = radicalized_peasantry
							duration = 3650
						}
					}
				}
				custom_tooltip = mop_great_peasants_war_permanent_pp_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					add_power_projection = {
						type = started_gpw_pp
						amount = 10
					}
				}
				change_peasant_zeal = {
					value = 20
				}
				add_faction_influence = {
					faction = abolitionists_faction
					influence = 25
				}
				add_dip_power = -100
				add_mil_power = -100
				hidden_effect = {
					emperor = {
						set_country_flag = early_gpw
						country_event = {
							id = great_peasant_war.1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
